## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a well-paced live workshop. Its two core frameworks, task/context/expectation (slides 26 to 27) and the five-step build recipe (slides 38 to 43), are each shown with one idea per slide, with a progress tag and a fill-in sentence on slide 39. The route is clear: orient, show workflows, teach prompting, practice in pairs, build, iterate, share. Weaknesses are consequential: many workflow slides (13 to 24, 59 to 61) pair a few lines of text with near-empty, illegible ChatGPT screenshots; section dividers and gradient filler slides (6, 12, 18, 20, 31, 32, 35, 46, 47, 53) add little; and the appendix text on slides 55 to 58 is small and dense.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the colour-coded task/context/expectation cards (slide 26), the build recipe table (38), and the stepped Build Recipe tags with the 'My assistant helps [who?] do [job?] by [process?]' template (39).

**Most useful repair:** Replace the washed-out, unreadable screenshots on slides 14, 15, 17, 19, 22, 24 and 59 to 61 with cropped, legible views or a concrete example prompt and output, and enlarge the small text on slides 55 to 58.

**What would change the score:** Cut or merge the decorative gradient dividers and the repeated Q+A, Resources and Thank you slides, and fix the layout glitches such as the empty half on slide 18 and the broken divider lines on slide 43.

_Confidence medium. 62 of 62 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide for an SME AI Accelerator workshop held in Abilene, TX on April 17, 2026, followed by introductions of three OpenAI staff and a stated goal: to get comfortable with ChatGPT, see practical education workflows, and leave ready to build an assistant. It then covers an agenda, getting started with ChatGPT, responsible use, and which product tiers exist, before moving through academic, study and project planning, and career readiness workflows. A prompting fundamentals section introduces a task, context, and expectation structure with a worked example and a pair activity. The middle section turns to building a reusable assistant, with a build recipe covering job definition, context, output format, guardrails and testing, followed by a hands-on build sprint, iteration clinic and share-out. The deck closes with feedback and community links, and an appendix describes ChatGPT Education and sample campus use cases. Slides worth studying include the task-context-expectation prompt framework and the five-step build recipe, which are presented as simple, reusable frameworks.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the OpenAI deck say about the five-step build recipe for a reusable assistant?**

Slide 38 lists the build recipe as five steps: 01 Define the job, 02 Add context & knowledge, 03 Specify the output, 04 Add guardrails, and 05 Test for reliability. Slides 39 through 43 then cover each step in detail.

**How many pages does the OpenAI deck have?**

The deck has 62 pages in total. It opens with a title slide for the SME AI Accelerator workshop held in Abilene, TX on April 17, 2026 (slide 1).

**What does the OpenAI deck say about the task, context, and expectation prompting structure?**

Slide 26, titled Get better results with clear prompting, asks what ChatGPT should do under Task, what it should know under Context, and what the answer should look like under Expectation. Slide 27 applies the same structure to a worked example about studying for a circuits exam.

**Which OpenAI product tiers or products does the deck introduce?**

Slide 9 says the session will focus on ChatGPT Plus and also lists ChatGPT Education, Codex, and the OpenAI API. It describes ChatGPT Education as institution-managed with enterprise-grade security, Codex as an AI tool that helps write and debug code, and the OpenAI API as a way to build custom solutions connected to your data.

Technical experts
After ChatGPT
AI is open to everyone
Students
Teachers
College faculty
Student services
Advising
Principals
District leaders
Academic affairs
Career services
Campus ops
7

Two equal columns split at the slide's vertical midline: a plain white left half and a full-bleed blue gradient right half. The title sits top-left at about 4% from the top and 2.7% from the left edge, with the left-column header below it. The right column's text is left-aligned to x 56.5%, with its body list in two sub-columns at x 56.5% and x 78.4%. One photograph-like background: a soft abstract blue gradient (not a subject photo) filling the right half, x 48.7% to 100%, full height. It is lighter pale blue (about #C2E4FB) at the top-right and bottom, with a deeper saturated sky blue (about #7EC8F2) band running diagonally from upper-left to lower-right through the middle right. No people, objects, charts or logos. A black filled rounded rectangle (About #000000, corner radius small, about 0.5% of slide height) sits behind 'After ChatGPT' at x 56.5%, y 21.3%, w 15.0%, h 6.6%. A thin black outlined rounded rectangle (1px stroke about #000000, no fill, corner radius small) sits behind 'Before ChatGPT' at about x 2.7%, y 21.2%, w 15.0%, h 6.7%.

Background: pure white #FFFFFF on the left half; blue gradient image on the right half. Titles and headers are black #000000 in OpenAI Sans (geometric grotesque sans-serif) Medium, with the bolded words 'limited to…' and 'everyone' in OpenAI Sans Bold. Body list text is black #000000 in OpenAI Sans Regular. Accent: solid black button #000000 with white #FFFFFF text (After ChatGPT) and black outline with black text (Before ChatGPT). Title and header size about 5.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.8%); body text about 3.2% of slide height; button labels about 2.5%; page number about 1.5% in Helvetica Neue. ### Slide 8

**Text**

Responsible use of AI
Follow your school or institution’s AI
policy
Get consent before sharing someone
else’s voice, work, or information
Review for accuracy, bias, tone, and
age-appropriateness
Protect sensitive student and staff data,
and know your data controls
Seek expert review for legal, medical, or
financial advice
Use Search or Deep Research when you
need current, cited information
OpenAI

**Layout**

Single slide with a left-aligned title at top-left (2.7% from left, 4.3% from top) and a large empty band beneath it. Six white rounded cards sit in a two-column, three-row grid: left column x 2.7 to 48.7%, right column x 51.3 to 97.3%, rows starting at y 34.2%, 53.9% and 73.6%, each 16.4% high with about 3.3% vertical gaps and about 2.6% horizontal gap. Each card has an icon at its left and two lines of left-aligned text. The OpenAI wordmark sits bottom-left at about x 2.7%, y 94.4%. No subtitle, no page number.

**Visuals**

Six black single-weight outline line icons, one per card, drawn with rounded strokes of about 2 px at 2000 px width. Card 1 (top-left): a simple person outline (head circle over a rounded shoulder arc). Card 2 (top-right): a notepad with binding rings on the left and a checkmark inside a rounded square. Card 3 (middle-left): an eye with a concentric circle iris. Card 4 (middle-right): a hexagon shield containing a person outline. Card 5 (bottom-left): a graduation mortarboard cap. Card 6 (bottom-right): a magnifying glass with a diagonal handle. All icons are black on white and unboxed. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient or pattern. Title and body text are pure black (#000000); there are no accent colours. Typeface is OpenAI Sans Medium, a geometric grotesque sans-serif, regular-looking weight for body and title. Title is about 5.4% of slide height; body is about 3.5% of slide height. Cards are white (#FFFFFF) rounded rectangles with a small corner radius (about 0.6% of slide width) and a soft, faint grey drop shadow (about #E4E4E4 blur) that lifts them off the white background. Cards have no border stroke. Icons are black (#000000) outline strokes.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Today, we’ll focus on ChatGPT Plus — but / needs grow / ChatGPT Education / Institution-managed / ChatGPT with / enterprise-grade security / Codex / AI tool that helps you write / and debug code / OpenAI API / Build custom solutions / connected to your data / OpenAI / Confidential and proprietary.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left in two lines, left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge and about 4.3% from the top. Below it, three equal-width rounded cards sit in one row, spanning the slide width with about 2.7% outer margins and about 2.5% gutters, each card about 29.8% wide and 58.9% tall, starting at about 29.8% from the top. Each card has its icon at top-left, a bold title about 60% down, and a two- or three-line description at the bottom. A footer with the OpenAI wordmark and a confidentiality line sits bottom-left at about 94% from the top. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three square blue gradient images, each cropped to a rounded-corner card. Card 1 (left) shows a soft pale-blue haze with a saturated blue diagonal band running from upper-left to lower-right and a pale highlight in the centre, with fine grain; it carries a solid black circle at its top-left corner. Card 2 (centre) shows a similar haze with a saturated blue diagonal band running from top-centre down to the lower-right, and carries a rounded-square app icon with a violet-blue cloud-like glyph containing a terminal prompt symbol, on a white rounded-square tile. Card 3 (right) shows the haze with a deeper blue band at the upper-right corner, and carries the black OpenAI knot logo at its top-left. Footer carries the small black OpenAI wordmark. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background. White background with pure black titles and body text and no accent colour other than the blue gradients. Title is OpenAI Sans Medium style sans-serif, normal weight, about 5.4% of slide height for each line. Card titles are bold Helvetica Neue style sans-serif at about 3.5% of slide height. Card descriptions are regular sans-serif at about 3.5% of slide height; card 1 description is set in OpenAI Sans Regular while cards 2 and 3 are set in Helvetica Neue Regular. Footer text is Helvetica Neue regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Cards have rounded corners of about 14 px on a 2000 px wide slide with no visible shadow or border. Card fills are gradients from pale blue #D6EEFA to saturated blue #6DB7F4 with grain texture.

**Text**

**Layout**

Three equal-width columns of text sit below a single left-aligned title at the top-left, with a left margin of about 2.7% of slide width. Each column has an icon, then a heading, then a bulleted list; column headings sit on a common baseline at about 27.5% from the top. The page number '10' sits near the bottom centre-right at about 70.6% from the top, overlapping the last line of the third column. The 'OpenAI' wordmark sits bottom-left at about 94.5% from the top.

**Visuals**

Three black single-weight line icons, each about 3% of slide width and 5% of slide height. (1) Students: a pen nib in an outlined pointed-shield shape with a dot at the tip, at about x 2.6%, y 17.3%. (2) Educators: a sunrise-like icon, a half-circle horizon line with three rays above and a small curved arc, at about x 34.7%, y 17.3%. (3) Staff & admin: two outlined user heads with a plus sign beside the second figure, at about x 67.4%, y 17.6%. No photographs, charts or diagrams. Icons are not boxed, shadowed or tinted.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title and headings are near-black (#0D0E10 for the title, #000000 for headings and body). Accents: none; the only colour is the black icon strokes. Typeface: OpenAI Sans, a geometric grotesque sans-serif; the title is Bold, headings and bullets are Regular. Title is about 4.4% of slide height; headings about 4.4%; body about 3.5%; page number about 1.5%. Bullets are a small round dot followed by a space, set with hanging lines that wrap to the left margin. No boxes, rules, shadows or corner radii.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed photographic background. A bold headline sits top-left in the left column with no title bar, left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge. A large white rounded card occupies the right column from about 41% to 95% horizontally and 2% to 80% vertically, containing three numbered steps (01, 02, 03) in a two-column sub-grid: numerals at about 43% x, step text at about 51% x, left-aligned. The slide number sits bottom-right at about 96% x, 94% y.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed abstract photograph of soft, blurred, flowing ribbon-like shapes in cyan, light blue, violet, magenta, teal and deep blue, with pale lavender at top-left and a dusty mauve-pink band at bottom-left. Smooth out-of-focus gradients, no people or objects recognisable. Behind a white rounded rectangle card (fill #FFFFFF, corner radius about 2% of slide width, no visible border or shadow) that spans x 41.2% to 95.3%, y 2.0% to 79.6%. No charts, tables or logos besides the text. The underlined text 'chat.com' is a hyperlink-style underline in black.

**Design**

Background is a blurred multicolour photographic gradient (cyan #4FC3F7 top-centre, violet #A57BE0 right, magenta #8E2A6B lower-right, dusty pink #B07A8A lower-left). Headline and step titles are black #000000 in OpenAI Sans Bold (headline) and OpenAI Sans Medium (step titles), sans-serif geometric grotesque; body text is OpenAI Sans Regular in black #000000; the prompt is OpenAI Sans Italic in black. Headline size about 5.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.8%); step titles about 5.4%; body text about 3.0%. Left-aligned throughout. No accent colours; flat black text on white card. Card has generous internal padding of about 4% of slide width.

**Review notes**

**Text**

Learn with ChatGPT / Ask anything. / • How do I prepare for this class? / • How do I teach this topic clearly? / • Give me ideas for this assignment... / OpenAI / 14

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white text column occupies the left 48.7% of the slide and a full-bleed blue image panel fills the right 51.3%. The title sits top-left at about 3.5% from the top and 2% from the left edge, left-aligned. The body text is left-aligned in the left column starting at about 24% of the height. The OpenAI wordmark sits bottom-left at about 94% of the height and the page number sits near the bottom of the right panel.

**Visuals**

One illustration: a screenshot-style mockup of the ChatGPT web interface, placed as a boxed portrait window (x 61.1%, y 7.2%, w 29.0%, h 85.7%) with rounded corners and a soft shadow, centred on a light blue gradient backdrop. The window shows a top bar with the ChatGPT logo at left, the label 'ChatGPT 5' with a dropdown chevron, and a restore-style icon at top right. A narrow left sidebar holds three small line icons (compose, search, and a stacked-squares icon). The centre reads 'What can I help with?' in dark grey sans-serif, with below it a rounded input bar containing a plus icon, the placeholder 'Ask anything', a microphone icon and a waveform icon. The bottom left has a small blue-violet circular icon, and the bottom centre has a tiny grey disclaimer line reading 'ChatGPT can make mistakes. OpenAI doesn't use OAI Customer Education workspace data to train its models.' The window background is off-white (about #FAFAFA) and the surrounding backdrop is a soft blur with light sky blue (about #8CCBF9) at left, pale blue (about #D6EEFD) at top right and a deeper cyan (about #5BC0F0) patch at right middle. Lighting is flat and even. The screenshot is not cropped and is fully visible within the window frame. One logo: the OpenAI wordmark in black at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left portion, with a soft light-blue gradient (#8CCBF9 to #D6EEFD to #5BC0F0) filling the right 51.3% of the slide. Clean minimal look. White background (#FFFFFF) on the left, soft blue gradient image on the right. Title and body text are black (#000000) in Helvetica Neue regular for the title and Helvetica Neue italic for bullets, with the bullet glyph in OpenAI Sans Italic. Title is about 5.4% of slide height, body about 4.7%, page number about 1.5%. Body uses roughly 1.3 line spacing with bullets hanging at about 2.3% from the left edge and text indented to about 4.2%. Accent colour is the blue gradient (#8CCBF9 to #5BC0F0). No visible rules or outlined shapes. The chat window has rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and a faint drop shadow.

Formatting rules
Use a concise writing style. No marketing fluff.
Every row must include at least one first-party source URL.
Keep the table readable (wrap text as needed).

Scoring rubric (apply consistently)
Business Impact (1–10): estimated time/cost saved, risk reduction, decision quality, or revenue enablement at the function level within 3–6 months.
Ease of Use (1–10): effort to adopt with existing ChatGPT Enterprise capabilities (setup complexity, policy/IT approvals, change management, training).
Output now.
If you cannot verify a department or a capability via first-party sources, exclude it, state why in “Coverage & Gaps,” and proceed.

High quality inputs will result in better output.
OpenAI

Header reads ChatGPT 4o > with a hamburger menu icon at left and a compose icon at right. A user message bubble in light grey (about #F0F0F0) with rounded corners reads: What is prompt engineering? / Describe in one sentence / followed by a few bullet points. The assistant reply begins with a small OpenAI logo mark, then the text Prompt engineering is the practice of designing and refining inputs (prompts) to maximize the quality, relevance, and accuracy of outputs from AI models., followed by five bullets: Involves crafting precise, clear, and structured instructions to guide AI behavior. / Utilizes contextual cues, examples, or constraints to elicit desired responses. / Often iterative, requiring testing and adjustment to achieve optimal results. / Critical for tasks such as text generation, data extraction, and decision support. / Plays a key role in leveraging AI models effectively for specific applications. Four cards are stacked top to bottom in the right panel at 43.2% left edge, 54.1% width and 18.5% height each, with gaps of about 4% between them, and a black round arrow button sits at the bottom-right corner of each card. **Visuals**

Four identical white rounded rectangles with soft blue drop shadows, each carrying one prompt and one black circular button with a white upward arrow at its lower right. Each card's blue shadow is a 1000 by 242 pixel image placed behind the card. No photographs, charts or diagrams. The black pill at top-left is a solid #000000 rounded rectangle with fully rounded ends (pill shape), with white monospaced text inside.

**Text**

write better prompts
“Meta-prompting” is used to write and refine more effective prompts.
Simply tell ChatGPT to…
➔ Help me write a Deep Research prompt
➔ Rewrite this prompt for another situation
➔ Change this prompt to get a clearer output
ChatGPT 5
What can I help with, Thom?
Ask anything
ChatGPT can make mistakes. OpenAI doesn't use OAI Customer Education workspace data to train its models.
OpenAI

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. The left column (about x 2 to 50) holds the title, body copy and three arrow bullets on a white field, with left margin about 2% and text top-aligned from y 3.5. The right column is a full-bleed blue gradient panel from x 48.7 to 100 (full height), with a ChatGPT app screenshot window centred within it at x 59.9 to 90.1 and y 5.4 to 94.5. The OpenAI wordmark sits bottom-left at about x 2.7, y 94.4. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style screenshot of the ChatGPT web interface, boxed (not full-bleed) inside a blue gradient panel. Subject: a blank new chat screen in a browser-like window with rounded corners and a soft shadow. Top bar: OpenAI knot logo at left, the model selector reading ChatGPT 5 with a downward chevron, and a small expand icon at right. Left edge sidebar with three small line icons stacked vertically: compose (pencil in square), search (magnifier), and library (stacked squares). Centre: the heading What can I help with, Thom? above a pill-shaped input bar with a plus icon, placeholder Ask anything, a microphone icon and a voice-waveform icon at right. Bottom: a small blue circular badge at left and a two-line grey disclaimer, centred. Lighting: flat, even UI lighting. Dominant colours: white window, light grey text, blue badge. Background panel: soft gradient from light sky blue #8FD0F8 at left and top to deeper cyan #5BC0F0 around the right-middle, fading to pale blue #DDF0FC at bottom right. No overlay or tint beyond the gradient. The window is not cropped. Also the OpenAI wordmark in black at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid white #FFFFFF on the left, with a blue gradient photograph (light sky blue #8FD0F8 to cyan #5BC0F0 to pale blue #DDF0FC) on the right from x 48.7 to 100. Clean white left field with black text and a full-bleed blue gradient photographic panel on the right. Title in HelveticaNeue regular black #000000 at about 5.4% of slide height, with the second line in OpenAI Sans Bold Italic black #000000 at the same size. Body text HelveticaNeue regular #000000 at about 3.2% of slide height, line spacing about 1.2. Bullets are HelveticaNeue Italic #000000 at 3.2%, each with a black arrow glyph (MS PGothic) at the left margin. Accent colour is the blue of the gradient panel, approx #5BC0F0, and the blue badge in the screenshot approx #3B4BE0. No shapes or rules. The screenshot window has white fill #FFFFFF, corner radius about 1% of width, and a faint drop shadow.

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A grid of normal, edge and missing-information test cases gives a clear visual checklist for testing an assistant's reliability.
- Review: Its two core frameworks, task/context/expectation (slides 26 to 27) and the five-step build recipe (slides 38 to 43), are each shown with one idea per slide, with a progress tag and a fill-in sentence on slide 39.
- What would change the score: Cut or merge the decorative gradient dividers and the repeated Q+A, Resources and Thank you slides, and fix the layout glitches such as the empty half on slide 18 and the broken divider lines on slide 43.

### Slide 45

**Text**

30 MINUTES / Try / Individual / Activity / Build sprint / Scan a QR code for copy/paste / instructions, conversation / starters, and test prompts. / Build individually or in pairs. / tinyurl.com/ai-skills-jam / Choose the GPT you want to build / Students / ● Study / Coach GPT / ● Career & / Transfer / Coach GPT / Educators / ● Lesson & / Materials / Builder / GPT / ● Family & / Student / Comms / Staff & admins / ● Student / Services / FAQ / Navigator / GPT / ● Leadership / Briefing / Copilot / GPT / OpenAI / 45

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a narrow white left column (about the left 32.5% of the width) holds the Try tag, the title block, the instructions, the QR code and the URL pill, with the OpenAI wordmark in the bottom-left corner. A full-height blue panel fills the right 67.5% and holds a headline and three equal white cards in a row, evenly spaced with about 1.5% gaps and bottoms aligned at about 94% of the height. Text is left-aligned throughout; margins are about 2.7% from the left edge and about 5% from the top. The page number sits inside the blue panel, hidden behind the middle card.

**Visuals**

Visual elements: (1) A black pill tag reading 30 MINUTES in white monospace type, with a light-blue circular icon to its right containing a black cursor-click icon (a pointer with radiating click marks), and the word Try beside it. (2) A square black-and-white QR code, about 431 by 431 pixels, boxed with no border, sharp corners, no crop, placed at the left of the URL pill; at its centre sits a small black rounded square containing an up-right arrow symbol that overlays the QR modules. (3) A blue pill-shaped button in #3D8DFF containing the white bold URL tinyurl.com/ai-skills-jam. (4) Three placed images, each 655 by 981 pixels, positioned under the three white cards; they are covered by the cards and only their soft shadow halo is visible around the card edges, so no image content can be read. (5) Three white rounded rectangles (cards) with soft drop shadows, each holding a bold heading and a bulleted list. No photographs or charts are present.

**Design**

Background is white on the left and a saturated blue #3D8DFF panel on the right. Titles and body text are black #000000; the headline and card-text on blue are white #FFFFFF; accents are #3D8DFF (URL pill and blue panel) and light blue #B9D6FF (icon circle). Typefaces: OpenAI Sans (a geometric grotesque sans-serif) in Medium for the title, subtitle and body, Bold for the URL, card headings and footer mark, and Regular for the bullet lists; the 30 MINUTES tag is set in RobotoMono Regular (monospace) with wide letter-spacing and the bullet glyphs use Arial. The page number sits bottom right at about 96.4% x, 94.4% y, and a small confidentiality line sits bottom centre-left at about 8.2% x, 94.5% y; no header, no visible grid.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed abstract photograph or 3D-render style image covering the entire slide: smooth, out-of-focus soft folds of material. A large pale-blue to cyan (#4DC4EA) field fills the upper left and centre. A lavender-to-violet (#B99CF2) curved ridge sweeps diagonally from the bottom centre up to the top right. A deep maroon (#5A0F2E) crease runs beside the ridge in the lower right, shading into a dark teal-blue (#1B7FAF) at the bottom right and a lighter teal (#3FB3D6) at the far bottom right corner. The lower left fades to a dusty mauve-brown (#8E6F80). Soft diffuse lighting, no people or objects, no overlay or tint, full-bleed with no cropping of the frame, no borders.

Page number and confidentiality line in Helvetica Neue regular sans-serif at about 1.5% of slide height. ### Slide 47

**Text**

Make it reliable
Iterate, improve,
and polish

**Layout**

Full-bleed single-slide layout with no grid or columns. The title sits left of centre, starting at about 16% from the left edge and vertically near the middle, left-aligned. The subtitle is right-aligned in the lower right, ending about 3% from the right edge. The small footer sits at the bottom left, about 8% from the left edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph-like abstract 3D render, full-bleed, covering the whole slide without cropping in a box. It shows smooth, soft-focus satin-like folds or ribbons of material. A pale cyan-blue surface fills the upper left and centre. A lilac-violet fold curves diagonally from the lower centre up to the upper right. A dark maroon-magenta shadow crease runs along the right side, and teal-blue surfaces appear at the lower right. Lighting is soft and diffused with gentle highlights on the lilac fold. The composition is shallow depth of field with blurred edges. Dominant colours are cyan #4CC9F0, lilac #B9A6F5, maroon #5A0F3A and teal-blue #1A7FB0. There is no overlay or tint beyond the natural colour. The title is also present as a raster image of the text 'Make it reliable' placed over the photograph, 2048 by 225 pixels. No logos or icons appear other than the footer text.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed abstract gradient render in cyan, lilac and maroon tones. All text is white. The title is set in a clean geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to OpenAI Sans), medium weight, sentence case, about 19% of slide height, set as an image. Subtitle text is OpenAI Sans Medium, sentence case, about 5.4% of slide height, white #FFFFFF, right-aligned on two lines. Footer is OpenAI Sans Regular, about 1.5% of slide height, white #FFFFFF. There are no visible shapes, rules, shadows or corner radii; the slide has a faint rounded outer edge from the image.

### Slide 49

**Text**

18 MINUTES / Try / Individual / Activity / Improve, test, and / save your solution / Improve your v1 / Test and retest with each change / Fix what breaks / Save it / Definition of Done: / ● Runs end-to-end / ● The output is usable / ● You've tested it / ● You saved it / OpenAI / Power-ups / Finished early? Try going up a level. / ● Add a full eval: 3 normal cases, 1 edge case, 1 missing info case / ● Add a tool: search, file upload, or image generation / 49

**Layout**

Two-column slide. The left column (about 0 to 32.5% of width) holds a header row at top with a black pill label and an icon, then a large two-line title, a two-line subtitle, a 'Definition of Done' block lower down, and the OpenAI wordmark at bottom left. The right column (about 32.5 to 100% width) is a solid blue panel holding four stacked white cards and a translucent power-ups box at the bottom. Left margin is about 2.7% of width; the top of the content sits at about 4.8% of height and the bottom at about 90%. There is no title placeholder in the usual top position; the title 'Individual Activity' sits below the header row. The page number '49' sits inside the power-ups box at about x 72%, y 70.6%.

**Visuals**

Icon: a small black sparkle/click-cursor glyph (a pointer arrow with three short radiating strokes) centred inside a light blue circle at about x 17.3%, y 4.8%, w 3.4%, h 6.0%. Blue circle fill about #BFD8FF. No photographs, charts or tables. Four white rounded cards and one translucent light-blue box sit on the blue panel, each with a soft blue-tinted drop shadow (the 1000 by 135 and 1000 by 275 pixel shadow images). Bullets are solid black filled dots.

**Design**

Background: white on the left, solid blue panel #3D8DFF covering the right third and a bit (x 32.5% to 100%). Title and body text black #000000. Accent: blue #3D8DFF for the panel, light blue #BFD8FF for the icon circle, power-ups box about #9EC6FF (white at 50% over blue). Typefaces: OpenAI Sans Medium for title and headings (geometric grotesque sans-serif), OpenAI Sans Regular for body and bullets, OpenAI Sans Bold for 'Power-ups', and Roboto Mono Regular in white for the '18 MINUTES' label (monospace, all caps). Title text is about 8.9% of slide height per line, subtitle about 5.4%, card text about 3.5%, body and bullets about 3.2%. Line and shape styles: cards are rounded rectangles with small corner radius (about 0.5% of height) and soft shadows; the power-ups box has a larger corner radius (about 2% of height). The pill has fully rounded ends. No gradients, no outlines.

### Slide 50

**Text**

What did
you build?

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed background. The title sits top-left in the left column with the subtitle directly beneath it, left-aligned at about 2% from the left edge. The right column holds four equal-width white cards stacked vertically, left edges at 48.7% of slide width, with about 2% gaps between cards and text inset about 3% inside each card. The left column below the subtitle is empty. A small page number '50' and a footer 'SME AI Accelerator' are placed near the bottom, both in white.

**Visuals**

One abstract photographic-style background illustration: a soft, blurred, iridescent gradient of flowing ribbon-like shapes. It covers the full slide (0 to 100% in both directions) and is not boxed or cropped to a frame. Colours run from pale lavender and sky blue at the top-left, through bright cyan in the upper middle, to violet and magenta streaks on the right, deep navy-teal at the lower right, and a dusty mauve and pink area at the lower left. Lighting is soft and diffuse, with brighter highlights near the top-centre. Four white rounded rectangles (cards) sit over the right side of the background as shapes, not images. No logos, icons, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background is a blurred multicolour gradient of cyan, lavender, violet, magenta and teal with no solid colour. The title and subtitle are white (#FFFFFF). Body and card headings are black (#000000). Cards are filled #FEFEFE with no visible border or shadow. Typeface is OpenAI Sans, a geometric sans-serif: Medium for the title and subtitle, Bold for card headings, Regular for card body text. Title size is about 8.9% of slide height, subtitle about 3.5%, card headings and body about 3.2%, footer and page number about 1.5%. Card corner radius is small, about 0.6% of slide width (roughly 12 px on a 2000 px wide slide). No gradients on the cards, no shadows, no accent colours.

The right area (about 57 to 100% of width) is a full-height blue gradient panel holding a white screenshot card centred vertically. Left margin about 2.5% of width; the OpenAI wordmark sits bottom-left and the page number 55 sits bottom-right at about 94% of height.

**Visuals**

Right panel: a full-bleed blue and white cloud-like gradient, mostly soft periwinkle blue #6F9BE8 at the top right and bottom right, fading to pale white-blue #DDE8FB along the left edge of the panel and the top left. Boxed on it is a white UI screenshot (827 by 974 pixels) of a chat interface titled Campus Help Desk, with a small circular cartoon avatar of a woman with dark hair in a dark blazer centred above the title, the subtitle By OAIEduDemo with a small person icon in grey, a three-line grey description paragraph, four rounded white prompt chips with thin light grey borders arranged in a row, and a grey message input bar with a paperclip icon at left and a grey circular send arrow at right, followed by a tiny grey disclaimer line. The screenshot is not cropped, is boxed with square corners and a faint shadow, and has no tint. Lighting is flat and even. No logos other than the small OpenAI wordmark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Left half solid white #FFFFFF; right panel blue-to-white cloud gradient from #6F9BE8 to #DDE8FB, covering the full slide. Clean white background on the left, with a blue gradient panel on the right. Titles and column headings are black #000000 and body text is black #000000. Accents are a thick black horizontal rule #000000 and a thin light grey rule #C8C8C8. Headline is a bold geometric grotesque sans-serif (OpenAI Sans Bold), about 4.4% of slide height. Column headings are a regular neo-grotesque sans-serif (Helvetica Neue), about 4.7% of slide height. Subheadings are Helvetica Neue Bold, about 2.0% of slide height. Body text is Helvetica Neue Regular, about 1.7% of slide height, with comfortable leading of roughly 2.4% of slide height. Page number is about 1.5% of slide height. The screenshot card has square corners and no visible border.

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: many workflow slides (13 to 24, 59 to 61) pair a few lines of text with near-empty, illegible ChatGPT screenshots; section dividers and gradient filler slides (6, 12, 18, 20, 31, 32, 35, 46, 47, 53) add little; and the appendix text on slides 55 to 58 is small and dense.

Formatting rules
Use a concise writing style. No marketing fluff.
Every row must include at least one first-party source URL.
Keep the table readable (wrap text as needed).
After the table, include a short “Methods & Citations” section listing all first-party sources used for features and department definitions with titles and (if available) last-updated dates.
Scoring rubric (apply consistently)
Business Impact (1–10): estimated time/cost saved, risk reduction, decision quality, or revenue enablement at the function level within 3–6 months.
Ease of Use (1–10): effort to adopt with existing ChatGPT Enterprise capabilities (setup complexity, policy/IT approvals, change management, training).
Output now.
If you cannot verify a department or a capability via first-party sources, exclude it, state why in “Coverage & Gaps,” and proceed.
Thanks for the clear and structured request. Before I begin, could you confirm a couple of things to make sure the output matches your expectations:
1. Should the use cases be focused exclusively on internal OpenAI departments (e.g., HR, Legal, Finance at OpenAI itself) or generalized for how any enterprise's departments might use ChatGPT Enterprise?
2. Do you want the top 3 use cases for each verified department, or only the single top use case per department?
3. Is there a minimum number of departments or table rows you expect? (e.g., if only 6 departments are publicly verifiable, is that acceptable?)
Once confirmed, I'll dive in and build the catalog per your strict sourcing and formatting requirements.
1. OpenAI specifically
2. For each verified department
3. No minimum
Research
Sources
ChatGPT can make mistakes. OpenAI doesn't use OAI Customer Education workspace data to train its models.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 8% from the top and 3% from the left edge. Five white rounded cards form a centred two-row grid: three cards across the upper row (x 9.8%, 38.1%, 65.6%, each 23.8% wide, y 29.0%, 20.6% tall) and two cards centred below (x 23.2%, 50.0%, y 56.3%). Footer runs along the bottom at about 94.5%: OpenAI wordmark at left, confidentiality line beside it, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five white rounded-rectangle cards with a single black outline icon above two lines of black italic text each. Card 1 (top left): a robot face outline with a square head, two dot eyes and a rounded-bottom body, labelled Student Services Front / Desk Assistant. Card 2 (top centre): a clipboard outline with a clip at top, labelled Registrar / & Policy Navigator. Card 3 (top right): a circular arrow outline with an arrowhead at upper right (refresh symbol), labelled Campus Comms / & Alert Builder. Card 4 (bottom left): a four-point sparkle cluster (one large and two small stars) in black outline, labelled Admissions & Events / Templates. Card 5 (bottom right): a rounded square outline with a check mark inside, labelled HR Onboarding / & Training Guide. Icons are about 5% of slide width and sit about 3% below the top of each card. Background is a full-bleed soft blue gradient image with lighter blue at top right and deeper sky blue at lower right, with no photographic subject.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed soft blue gradient image, pale sky blue #BCE2FD at top right fading to saturated sky blue #7DC9F6 at lower right and light blue #A9D8FA at left.
